Compute `_autocorr_pos(a)` with FFT:
```
def _autocorr_pos(a):
    n = len(a)
    N = _next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:2*n-1]
    return c[n-1:]  # length n
```
This gives positive lags r_d. Need ensure real and small negative clipped? For nonnegative, should be nonnegative; numerical can be tiny negative. Use `np.maximum(c,0)`? We can combine in a function that returns u and weighted gradient given a and p, with one A FFT and one W FFT:
```
def _autocorr_and_grad_u(a, p, need_grad=True):
    n=len(a); N=...
    A=rfft(a,N)
    c=irfft(A*A,N)[:2n-1]
    r=c[n-1:2n-1]
    u=n*r
    if need_grad:
       W=rfft(p,N)
       left=irfft(W*A,N)[:n]
       ar=a[::-1]
       Ar=rfft(ar,N)
       right=irfft(W*Ar,N)[n-1:2n-1]
       grad_u=n*(left+right)
    return u, grad_u
```

Line search mixing:
```
def _mix_search(a, g, t_values):
   best=a, best_sc=score(a)
   for t in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0]:
       b=(1-t)*a + t*g; b=project; sc=score_offpeak(b)
       if sc<best_sc: update
   return best
```

def _min_cross_g(a, max_iter=300, beta=0.05, lr=0.05, deadline=..., min_rem=5, seed=0):
    n=len(a)
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    # choose start: uniform, a, random smooth
    g = np.ones(n)/n
    for iter:
       X = n * _cross_pos(a,g)  # length n
       u = X[1:]? include d=1..n-1
       # if all zeros, break
       umax = max
       shift = (X - umax)/beta
       exp = np.exp(np.clip(shift[1:], -700, 0))  # ignore d=0
       p = zeros(n); S=exp.sum(); if S>... p[1:]=exp/S else top
       grad = n * _convolve(p,a)[:n]  # _fft_convolve(p,a)
       tangent = grad - mean(grad)
       gn = norm; if small break
       # adaptive step
       step = lr / (gn + 1e-12)
       g = project_simplex(g - step * tnorm?) Wait tangent already scaled? If divide by gn, direction unit; step maybe 0.02. 
```

Mixing line search:
```
def _mix_best(a,g,old_sc, t_list):
   best=a; best_sc=old_sc
   for t in t_list:
      b=(1-t)*a+t*g
      b=_project_simplex(b)
      sc=_score_offpeak(b)
      if sc<best_sc: best,best_sc=b,sc
   return best,best_sc
```
Try t list [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5]. Use `old_sc` from current. If g cross good, small t improve. If t too large worsens.

```
class _SelfProb:
   def __init__(self,beta,n): self.beta=beta; self.n=n; self._last=None; self._last_grad=None
   def __call__(self,z):
       val, grad = _self_smooth_calc(z,self.beta) # top-level returns both
       self._last=z; self._last_grad=grad
       return val
   def grad(self,z):
       if self._last is not None and np.array_equal(z,self._last): return self._last_grad
       val, grad = _self_smooth_calc(z,self.beta)
       return grad
```

Detailed functions:

```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
    return a/s
```

Implement `_fft_convolve(a,b)`: for speed, if len small maybe use np.convolve? For n small detection? We use internal for large. FFT overhead small; for n<256 direct may be faster. Implement:
```
def _fft_convolve(a,b):
    n=len(a); m=len(b); out=n+m-1
    if out < 1024:
       return np.convolve(a,b)
    N=_next_pow2(out)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); B=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*B,N)[:out]
```
But many calls for same length; using direct for small. For large, FFT.

Potential issue: np.fft.rfft on float64 real; result complex. Multiplication A*A for autocorr. irfft returns float64 with tiny imag discarded. Good.

Project simplex fast:
```
def _project_simplex(x):
    n=len(x)
    x=x.copy()
    u=np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
    cond = (cssv - 1.0) / idx < u
    rho = np.where(cond)[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    r=rho[-1]
    theta=(cssv[r]-1.0)/(r+1.0)
    return np.maximum(x-theta,0.0)
```
Could be slow due sorting. We can use `_project_simplex_fast` with a linear approximation? There is algorithm using binary search on theta after sorting? Sorting still. Maybe we can avoid projection by log-domain for most, and only projection in direct subgrad. Direct subgrad fewer iterations. L-BFGS no projection. Cross direct projection each iter. Okay.

But cross direct max_iter maybe 200, sorting 200*16k log ~ maybe 1s. Fine.

Self subgrad if 5000 iterations sorting 5000*16k log ~? Sorting 16k ~0.003s, 5000=15s. okay.

Now, L-BFGS with endpoints and softmax. Need top-level class:
```
class _SelfSmoothing:
    def __init__(self,beta,n,incL,incR): ...
    def __call__(self,z):
        val, grad = _self_smooth_calc(z,self.beta,self.n,self.incL,self.incR)
        self._last=z; self._last_grad=grad; return val
    def grad(self,z):
        if self._last is not None and np.array_equal(z,self._last): return self._last_grad
        val, grad = _self_smooth_calc(z,self.beta,self.n,self.incL,self.incR)
        return grad
```
Top-level `_self_smooth_calc(z,beta,n,incL,incR)`:

idx=0
P[idx:idx+n-1]=u[1:]; idx+=n-1

# p_d from e first n-1 /S

if incL: grad[0]+=2*n*wl*a[0]
if incR: grad[-1]+=2*n*wr*a[-1]
gdot=np.dot(grad,a)
zgrad=a*(grad-gdot)

```

`_self_subgrad` details:
```
def _self_subgrad(a_init, deadline, min_rem, incL, incR, max_iter=5000, beta=0.05, seed=0):
    a=_normalize(a_init).copy(); n=len(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_internal(a,incL,incR)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    v=np.zeros(n)
    momentum=0.8; lr=0.03; beta_cur=beta; no_improve=0; st=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.monotonic()+min_rem>deadline: break
       maxP,S,p,wl,wr,grad = _self_weights_and_grad(a,beta_cur,incL,incR)
       tangent=grad - np.mean(grad)
       gn=np.linalg.norm(tangent)
       if gn < 1e-14: break
       eff=lr/gn * 0.2  # L2 step 0.2? maybe too large. Use 0.05.
       v = momentum*v - eff*tangent
       cand=a + v
       cand=_project_simplex(cand)
       a=cand
       beta_cur=max(0.002, beta_cur*0.997)
       lr=min(0.05, lr*1.001)
       # every 5 check score
       if it % 5 ==0 or it==max_iter-1:
          sc=_score_internal(a,incL,incR)
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
             best_sc=sc; best=a.copy(); no_improve=0
          else: no_improve+=1
          if no_improve>150:
             # perturb
             pert=0.001*rng.standard_normal(n)
             cand=_project_simplex(a+pert)
             pcand=_score_internal(cand,incL,incR)
             if pcand < best_sc: best=cand; best_sc=pcand
             a=cand; v=np.zeros(n); lr=0.03; no_improve=0
             st+=1
             if st>8: break
    return best,best_sc
```

Let's set `effective = lr / max(gn, 1e-12); effective = min(0.05, max(1e-5, effective))`. v momentum. At uniform, grad for lag1: components near 1? Ln norm maybe sqrt(n) ~128, effective=1e-4? def _cross_mix_loop(a_init, deadline, min_rem, incL, incR, seed=0, max_outer=10, inner_it=120, beta=0.1):
    a=_normalize(a_init).copy()
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_internal(a,incL,incR)
    t=0.1; no_improve=0
    for outer in range(max_outer):
        if time... break
        # maybe adapt beta based on score
        g, xmax = _min_cross_g(a, max_iter=inner_it, beta=beta, deadline=deadline, min_rem=min_rem, seed=seed+outer, incL=incL, incR=incR)
        b, sc = _mix_best(a, g, best_sc, incL, incR, [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5])
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc=sc; best=b.copy(); no_improve=0; t=min(0.4,t*1.2)
        else:
            no_improve+=1
            if t>0.02: t*=0.7
            else:
                # perturb a a bit
                if no_improve>4: break
        if outer%2==0:
            # optional small self subgrad between? no
    return best,best_sc

def _min_cross_g(a_fixed, max_iter, beta, deadline, min_rem, seed, incL, incR):
    n=len(a_fixed)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    starts=[]
    starts.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    starts.append(_normalize(a_fixed).copy())
    # random smooth start
    nz=... generate small
    starts.append(nz)
    best_g=None; best_x=np.inf
    iters_per=max(20, max_iter//len(starts))
    for si,g0 in enumerate(starts):
       g=_normalize(g0).copy()
       v=zeros; lr=0.015; beta_cur=beta; no=0
       for it in range(iters_per):
          if time... break
          Xmax,S,p,wl,wr,grad = _cross_weights_and_grad(a_fixed,g,beta_cur,incL,incR)
          if it%5==0:
             if Xmax < best_x: best_x=Xmax; best_g=g.copy()
          if Xmax < 1e-8: break
          tangent=grad-mean; gn=norm
          if gn <1e-13: break
          eff=lr/max(gn,1e-13); eff=min(0.05,eff)
          # use small momentum? no
          g=_project_simplex(g - eff*tangent)
          beta_cur=max(0.02, beta_cur*0.98)
          # if no improvement? use Xmax
       after loop final X
       if Xmax < best_x: update
    if best_g is None: best_g=np.ones(n)/n
    return best_g,best_x
```

`_mix_best`:
```
def _mix_best(a,g,base_sc,incL,incR,t_list=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5)):
    a=_normalize(a); g=_normalize(g)
    best=a; best_sc=base_sc
    for t in t_list:
       b=(1.0-t)*a + t*g
       b=_project_simplex(b)
       sc=_score_internal(b,incL,incR)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best=b; best_sc=sc
    return best,best_sc
```

`_self_weights_and_grad(a,beta,incL,incR)`:

# P values length m
m=n-1 + int(incL)+int(incR)
P=np.empty(m)
if n>1: P[:n-1]=r[1:]*n
idx=n-1
if incL: P[idx]=n*a[0]*a[0]; idx+=1
if incR: P[idx]=n*a[-1]*a[-1]; idx+=1
maxP=P.max()
shifted=(P-maxP)/beta
e=np.exp(np.clip(shifted,-700,0))
S=e.sum()
p=np.zeros(n)
if n>1 and S>0: p[1:]=e[:n-1]/S
wl=wr=0
if incL: wl=e[n-1]/S
if incR: wr=e[idx-1]/S
grad = n*_autograd_r(p,a)
if incL and wl>0: grad[0]+=2*n*wl*a[0]
if incR and wr>0: grad[-1]+=2*n*wr*a[-1]

```

`_SelfSmoothing` in class can use `_self_smooth_value` and `_self_smooth_grad`; it may compute P twice per main iteration if fun then grad. Could cache as earlier. Implement:
```
class _SelfSmoothing:
    def __init__(...): self._z=None; self._val=None; self._grad=None
    def __call__(self,z):
        # If z same as last, return cached value, but line search different z compute.
        if self._z is not None and (z is self._z or np.array_equal(z,self._z)):
            return self._val
        val=_self_smooth_value(z,...)
        self._z=z; self._val=val; self._grad=None
        return val
    def grad(self,z):
        if self._grad is not None and self._z is not None and (z is self._z or np.array_equal(z,self._z)):
            return self._grad
        grad=_self_smooth_grad(z,...)
        # If we didn't have val, compute? not needed.
        self._z=z; self._grad=grad
        return grad
```
But if `__call__` computes value only, then grad computes full again. For main iteration, scipy calls fun then grad at same z; we can cache a full calc in `__call__`? If `__call__` computes both val and grad and stores, line search fun at trial cannot use grad. But main z after line search: scipy calls fun at current, then grad? It may call grad first? To save, in `__call__` we can compute value AND grad if a flag `self._want_grad`? Can't know. Simpler: not cache across fun/grad; cost okay with conservative maxiter. Or in `__call__` compute only value; gradient separate. L-BFGS line search calls fun many; each fun autocorr only (no gradient convs) cheaper. Good. Separate value and grad functions: value requires autocorr and P/S; grad requires autocorr, P/S, and two convs. This is efficient. We'll do that.

`_lbfgs_self`:
```
def _lbfgs_self(a_init, deadline, min_rem, incL, incR):
    a=_normalize(a_init).copy(); n=len(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_internal(a,incL,incR)
    betas=[0.1,0.05,0.025,0.0125,0.006,0.003]
    for i,beta in enumerate(betas):
       remaining=deadline-min_rem-time.monotonic()
       if remaining < 30: break
       z=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
       prob=_SelfSmoothing(beta,n,incL,incR)
       # max_iter based on remaining and beta stage. Suppose value cost 0.03, grad cost 0.08, line search 3 -> 0.17. Use 0.2.
       max_iter = min(600, max(40, int(remaining / 0.25)))
       # For later smaller remaining, smaller.
       try:
          res=minimize(prob, z, jac=prob.grad, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':max_iter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-9,'maxcor':10,'maxls':10})
          a_new=_softmax(res.x)
          sc=_score_internal(a_new,incL,incR)
          if sc<best_sc-1e-12:
              best=a_new; best_sc=sc
       except Exception: pass
    return best,best_sc
```
Potential `prob.grad` method signature expects `(z)`, good. `minimize` calls fun(x) and jac(x). `prob.grad` as method works. But `fun` and `jac` bound methods; class top-level.
Option `gtol=1e-9` maybe early stop. Good.

Now, generation of random smoothed starts:
```
def _random_smooth_start(n, rng, alpha=0.8):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(noise)
    # keep low frequencies
    cut=max(1,int(n*0.05))
    A[cut:]*=0.0
    noise=np.fft.irfft(A,n)
    # normalize via tanh
    h=1.0 + alpha*np.tanh((noise - np.median(noise))*2.0)
    # apply sine taper to reduce endpoints if needed
    taper=np.sin(np.pi*x)
    h=h * (0.1 + 0.9*taper)
    h=np.maximum(h,1e-6)
    return _normalize(h)
```
Random may have edge taper. Multiple seeds.

Cosine modulated taper:
```
f = rng.choice([3,5,7,11,17,23]) ; phi=rng.uniform(0,2pi); h=(1+0.8*np.cos(2π f x + phi))* (0.2+0.8*np.sin(π x)); clip.
```
Sine profiles: `h=np.sin(π x)^p` for p [0.5,1,1.5,2,3]. For p=0.5 edges sqrt sin.

Parabolic: `h=1 - 0.9*(2x-1)^2` -> edge 0.1, center1. Also `h=1 - 0.95*(2x-1)^4`.

Maybe "opposite phase" with very low lag: Use `h = 1 + α cos(2π f x)` with f chosen to make high values separated. But uniform modulation autocorr period. Multiply taper.

Check `_fft_convolve` for arrays maybe length 0? Ensure n>=1.
```
def _fft_convolve(a,b):
    n=len(a); m=len(b)
    if n==0 or m==0: return np.array([])
    out=n+m-1
    if out <= 2048:
        return np.convolve(a,b)
    N=_next_pow2(out)
    try:
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); B=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
       return np.fft.irfft(A*B,N)[:out]
    except Exception:
       return np.convolve(a,b)
```
Direct for small. For many calls same N, okay.

`_softmax`: To handle z too large: subtract max; clip.
```
def _softmax(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
   z=z-np.max(z)
   e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-700,0))
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.ones(len(z))/len(z)
   return e/s
```

```

def _detect_objective():
   ntests=[15,23,31]
   czero=lzero=rzero=ctry=0
   nonzeroL=nonzeroR=0
   for n in ntests:
      ct=n//2
      sl=_probe_spike(n,0); sc=_probe_spike(n,ct); sr=_probe_spike(n,n-1)
      ctry+=1
      if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < 1e-6: czero+=1
      elif np.isnan(sc): pass
      if np.isfinite(sl) and sl < 1e-6: lzero+=1
      else: nonzeroL += 1
      if np.isfinite(sr) and sr < 1e-6: rzero+=1
      else: nonzeroR += 1

class _SelfSmoothing:
    def __init__(self, beta, n, incL, incR):
        self.beta = beta
        self.n = n
        self.incL = incL
        self.incR = incR
    def __call__(self, z):

    def grad(self, z):
        return _self_smooth_grad(z, self.beta, self.n, self.incL, self.incR)

`_grad_u_weighted(p,a)` returns n*(left+right). We can define:
```
def _grad_u_weighted(p,a):
   n=len(a)
   if n <=1: return np.zeros(n)
   left = _fft_convolve(p,a)[:n]
   ar = a[::-1]
   right = _fft_convolve(p,ar)[n-1:2*n-1]
   out = left + right
   return n * out
```
This computes two convs each call. For gradient self many times. Could optimize by doing one FFT for left/right? Maybe not.

`_self_smooth_grad`:

if incL and wl>0: grad[0]+=2*n*wl*a[0]
if incR and wr>0: grad[-1]+=2*n*wr*a[-1]
gdot=float(np.dot(grad,a))
zgrad=a*(grad-gdot)
return zgrad
```

Now, start generation details no lambda:
```
def _generate_starts(n, rng):
    starts=[]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    # uniform
    starts.append(np.ones(n))
    # sine powers
    for p in (0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0):
        h=np.sin(np.pi*x)**p
        if np.sum(h)>0: starts.append(h)
    # parabolic
    for c in (0.5,0.8,0.95):
        u=2*x-1
        h=1.0 - c*u*u
        if np.min(h)>0 or True: starts.append(np.maximum(h,1e-3))
        h4=1.0 - c*u**4; starts.append(np.maximum(h4,1e-3))
    # random smooth 3
    for k in range(3):
        h=_random_smooth_at(n,rng,0.7+0.1*k)
        starts.append(h)
    # modulated taper
    for f in (3,5,7,11,17):
        ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
        h=(1.0 + 0.8*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x + ph)) * (0.15 + 0.85*np.sin(np.pi*x))
        h=np.maximum(h,1e-3)
        starts.append(h)
    # normalize all and remove duplicates? Not necessary.
    out=[]
    for h in starts:
        hh=_normalize(h)
        out.append(hh)
    return out
```
Need `_random_smooth_at` top-level.

`_random_smooth_at(n,rng,alpha)`:

A=np.fft.rfft(noise)

A[cut:]*=0.0
low=np.fft.irfft(A,n)
# add high-frequency random clipped positive? 
med=np.median(low)
h=1.0 + alpha*np.tanh(low*2.0)
# ensure nonnegative
h=np.maximum(h,0.02)
# random phase chirp optional

    f=rng.integers(1,7)
    h += 0.3*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*np.arange(n)/n)

```

Let's sketch `propose_candidate`:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=1800):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try: B=max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    except: B=1800.0
    deadline=t0+B-10.0
    if deadline < t0+5.0: deadline=t0+5.0
    seed=int(seed) % (2**63-1)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent=_load_parent()
    exclude, incL, incR = _detect_objective()

    # choose n
    n=16383
    if parent is not None:
        lp=len(parent)
        if 4096 <= lp <= 32768:
            n=lp
        elif lp < 4096:
            n=4096
        else:
            n=32768? But large maybe slow. Use 16383.
    # maybe ensure n not too large for evaluation speed: cap 24576? If parent 32768, too slow. Use 16383.
    if n > 32768: n=16383
    if n < 1024: n=16383

    # If full and no parent indication, return uniform.
    parent_act = _actual_score(parent) if parent is not None else np.inf
    if not exclude:
        if parent_act < 1.999:
            exclude=True
        else:
            # uniform maybe length 64
            return [1.0]*64

    # Build starts
    starts=[]
    if parent is not None:
        starts.append(_resample(parent,n))
    if parent is None:
        starts.append(np.ones(n)/n) # generate will include
    starts.extend(_generate_starts(n,rng))

    scored=[]
    for idx,a in enumerate(starts):
        a=_normalize(a)
        sc=_score_internal(a,incL,incR)
        scored.append((sc, idx, a))
    scored.sort()

    best_seq=scored[0][2]
    best_int=scored[0][0]
    best_act=_actual_score(best_seq)
    # candidates list? We can maintain `alt_candidates` list of (act,int,seq,tag)
    alts=[]
    if parent is not None:
       alts.append((_actual_score(parent), _score_internal(_normalize(parent),incL,incR), _normalize(parent), 'parent'))
```

Let's design cross solver:
```
def _min_cross_full(p, max_iter, beta, deadline, min_rem, seed):
   n=len(p)
   best_q=p.copy(); best_x=score_scaled? (max n*conv(p,p))
   # multiple starts? uniform and p and maybe random smooth.
   for start in [p, uniform, random_taper]:
      q=normalize(start)
      v? no
      lr=0.02
      beta_cur=beta
      for it in range(iter_per):
         if time...
         u = n*full_conv(p,q)  # length L
         maxu = max(u)
         if maxu < best_x: best...
         shifted=(u-maxu)/beta_cur
         e=exp; S; w=e/S
         h=corr(w,p)  # length n
         grad = n*h
         tang = grad - mean(grad)
         gn=norm
         step = lr/max(gn,1e-12); step=min(0.05,step)
         q=project(q - step*tang)
         beta_cur=max(0.01,beta_cur*0.98)
   return best_q,best_x
```
But computing `full_conv(p,q)` and `corr(w,p)` each iter: first FFT p_len n, q_len n -> N 2n; second weights length L and p_rev -> N 3n. ~3 FFT. 200 iter maybe 10s. Good.

Their `_conv_grad_single(a,k=1)`: first part j_lo=max(0,1-1)=0 j_hi=min(1,1)=1: idx=k-arange(0,2)=[1,0] -> grad[0]=a1, grad[1]=a0. second: j_lo2=max(0,1)=1, j_hi2=min(1,2)=1: idx=[1]-1=0 -> grad[1]+=a0. Total [b,2a]? But true grad of 2ab is [2b,2a]. Missing factor at j=0. So previous internal gradient was wrong for full conv! Yet it achieved 1.505? Maybe because they also used L-BFGS `_smograd` which may have correct? Let's check `_smograd`: It used `wc = _full_convolve(w, a_rev); h = wc[n-1:2n-1]; W=dot(w,c); grad=4 n a*(h-W)`. This is the correct soft gradient for full conv through softmax? Let's verify for n=2 uniform? It may be correct. Their subgradient phase may have been suboptimal but L-BFGS correct. Good.

Our derived h=corr(w,a) is correct. Great.

Now, need ensure gradient formula for self L-BFGS: g_a=2n h. For n=2, c=[a²,2ab,b²], u=n c. Suppose weights w, h_j = ∑ w_k a_{k-j}: h0=w0a0+w1a1; h1=w1a0+w2a1. g_a=2n h = [2n(w0a0+w1a1), 2n(w1a0+w2a1)]. True derivative of n∑w c: c0 a² ->2n w0 a0; c1 2ab ->2n w1 b; c2 b² ->2n w2 b. For a0: 2n w0 a0 + 2n w1 b. matches. Good.

Now, cross gradient for q: g_q=n h. For n=2, c1 derivative wrt q0 = n*w1*a1? X1=n*2 p0 q1? Wait full conv p*q: c1=2 p0 q1. X1=n*c1. derivative wrt q1 =2n p0. Our h for q: j=1 (q1): ∑ w_k p_{k-1}: k=1 p0. g=n w1 p0. Missing factor 2! Because full conv p*q coefficient c1 includes two terms for non-symmetric? In linear convolution of p and q, c_k =∑ p_i q_{k-i}; for p,q different, there is no double counting except if terms distinct? For n=2, c1 = p0 q1 + p1 q0; derivative wrt q1 = p0 only, not 2p0. But if q=p and we differentiate c(p,p), c1=2p0p1 because both variables same and two terms. For cross fixed p variable q, c1= p0 q1 + p1 q0, derivative wrt q1=p0. Thus cross gradient = n*h (not 2). Good. I earlier thought c1=2ab for p=q; for p,q different it's sum of two terms. The formula h_j=∑w_k p_{k-j} gives for j=q1 (index1): k=1 p0, only one. Correct. For self p,q same, derivative has factor 2 because q_j and p_j both change; if q=p and we consider derivative of F(p,p) wrt p, it's 2 times derivative of cross F(p,q) at q=p. So self gradient 2n h, cross n h. Good.

Now, MM mixing: c_b = (1-t)² c_p + 2t(1-t) c_{p,q} + t² c_q. In linear convolution, cross c_{p,q} at lag includes ordered terms, and both cross terms (p*q and q*p) equal? Since convolution commutative, c_{p,q}=c_{q,p}. The expansion of b*b has 2 p*q. Good. Cross objective min max X = n*c_{p,q}. For current p, q=p gives X=M. min ≤M.

Now, the previous LP state: maximize sum b subject to f*b≤M. With f sum=√2n, M maybe. Here q simplex. Fine.

Now, can we derive a direct linear update? Cross min q can be seen as minimizing max linear. We can solve with projected gradient. Good.

Let's define `_score_full(p)` exactly like evaluate but internal. Use `sum(p)`? Normalize. For speed, assume normalized? Still check.
```
def _score_full(p):
    p=_normalize(p)
    n=len(p)
    if n<1: return np.inf
    c=_fft_convolve(p,p)
    m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*m)
```
Since p sum1.

`_actual_score` as before.

`_logsp_weights(u,beta)`:
```
m=float(np.max(u))
sh=(u-m)/beta
e=np.exp(np.clip(sh,-700,0))
S=float(e.sum())
if S<=0 or not finite:
    w=np.zeros_like(u); w[int(np.argmax(u))]=1.0; S=1
else: w=e/S
return m,S,w
```

`_corr_weights(w,p)` as above. Need ensure w length =2n-1. If not maybe pad? Use:
```
def _corr_weights(w,p):
    n=len(p)
    rv=p[::-1]
    out=_fft_convolve(w,rv)
    return out[n-1:2*n-1]
```
If out shorter, pad? But should.

Class `_SelfSoftFun` with methods no cache? Cache maybe. We can implement simple no cache; or cache value/grad to reduce. Let's implement cache similar but simple:
```
class _SelfSoft:
  def __init__(self,beta): self.beta=beta; self._z=None; self._val=None; self._grad=None
  def __call__(self,z):
     # if same z as last grad, maybe value cached
     if self._z is not None and (z is self._z or np.array_equal(z,self._z)):
        if self._val is not None: return self._val
     self._val=_self_smooth_z_value(z,self.beta)
     self._z=z; self._grad=None
     return self._val
  def grad(self,z):
     if self._grad is not None and self._z is not None and (z is self._z or np.array_equal(z,self._z)): return self._grad
     self._grad=_self_smooth_z_grad(z,self.beta)
     self._z=z
     # recompute value if needed? not
     return self._grad
```
But if `__call__` called at z, then grad at same z: value stored, grad computes. If grad called first, value None; okay. However if `__call__` called after grad for same z, `self._val` None but `self._grad` not None; it will recompute value. Could store val in grad too: in grad, compute a common calc returning val,grad to reuse. Simpler: define `_self_smooth_z_both(z,beta)` returning val,grad. Then class __call__ calls both and stores; line search fun wastes grad? But if line search calls fun many, computing both heavy. Maybe no cache. Simplicity > performance? L-BFGS not main maybe. Use no cache but separate value/grad. Conservative iterations.

Given time, we can include cross direct and self subgrad, and L-BFGS with no cache. Good.

  except: pass
return best,best_sc
```

Let's detail `_cross_min(p, max_iter, beta, deadline, min_rem, seed)`:
```
n=len(p); p=_normalize(p)
rng=default_rng(seed)
starts=[]; starts.append(p.copy()); starts.append(np.ones(n)/n)
# maybe an edge-asymmetric start: ramp 2x? h=i+1? normalize.
h=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n); h=np.maximum(h,1e-4); starts.append(h)
h=1.0-np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n); starts.append(h)
# random smooth
starts.append(_random_smooth_at(n,rng,0.7))
it_per=max(20, max_iter//len(starts))
best_q=p.copy(); best_x=np.inf
for si,g0 in enumerate(starts):
  q=_normalize(g0).copy()
  lr=0.02; beta_cur=beta; no=0
  for it in range(it_per):
    if time.monotonic()+min_rem>deadline: break
    u=n*_fft_convolve(p,q)
    maxu,S,w=_logsp_weights(u,beta_cur)
    if maxu < best_x:
       best_x=maxu; best_q=q.copy()
    h=_corr_weights(w,p)
    grad=n*h
    tang=grad - float(np.mean(grad))
    gn=np.linalg.norm(tang)
    if gn <1e-14: break
    step=lr/max(gn,1e-14)
    if step>0.05: step=0.05
    q=_project_simplex(q - step*tang)
    beta_cur=max(0.01, beta_cur*0.97)
  # final update best
  u=n*_fft_convolve(p,q); mx=float(np.max(u))
  if mx<best_x: best_x=mx; best_q=q.copy()
return best_q, best_x
```
Note `best_x` is max scaled u (not score/2). Compare with self score/2. For current p, self M_scaled = max(n*conv(p,p)). Cross min max X ≤ M.

Line search mix `_mix_best_full(p,q,base_score/2?)`:
```
def _mix_best_full(p,q,base_m): # base_m = maxcaled current? 
  p=_normalize(p); q=_normalize(q); n=len(p)
  best=p; best_m=base_m
  for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.8):
     b=(1-t)*p+t*q
     b=_project_simplex(b)
     u=n*_fft_convolve(b,b); m=float(np.max(u))
     if m<best_m: best_m=m; best=b
  return best,best_m,2*best_m
```
Use scaled max to avoid overflow.

`_cross_mm_loop`:
```
def _cross_mm_loop(p_init, deadline, min_rem, seed=0, max_outer=30, inner_it=80, beta=0.08):
   p=_normalize(p_init).copy()
   current_m=float(np.max(n*full_conv(p,p)))
   best=p; best_m=current_m
   no=0
   for outer in range(max_outer):
      if time... break
      q,xmin=_cross_min(p,max_iter=inner_it,beta=beta,deadline=deadline,min_rem=min_rem,seed=seed+outer)
      b,bm,_=_mix_best_full(p,q,current_m)
      if bm < best_m - 1e-12:
          best_m=bm; best=b.copy(); no=0
          # maybe update p to b
          p=best.copy()
          current_m=best_m
      else:
          no+=1
          # try random q start? or reduce beta
          if no>5: break
   return best,best_m,2*best_m
```
After each improvement, p updated. Maybe line search t small; if no improvement, try smaller t? `_mix_best` already tries. If no improvement, break. Could also perturb p.

```
def _generate_starts(n,rng):
    starts=[]
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    starts.append(np.ones(n))
    starts.append(x+0.02)
    starts.append(1.0 - x +0.02)
    starts.append(x*x +0.02)
    starts.append((1-x)*(1-x)+0.02)
    starts.append((x-0.5)**2 +0.02)
    # edge bumps spread:
    for width in [0.1,0.2,0.3]:
        h=0.1 + np.exp(-((x/width)**2)) + np.exp(-(((1-x)/width)**2))
        starts.append(h)
    # cosine modulated with taper? full conv not need taper? But nonnegative.
    for f in [3,5,7,11,17]:
        ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
        h=1.0 + 0.9*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x + ph)
        starts.append(np.maximum(h,0.01))
    # random smooth
    for k in range(4):
        starts.append(_random_smooth_at(n,rng,0.6+0.15*k))
    out=[]; seen? no
    for h in starts:
       hh=_normalize(h); if hh is not None: out.append(hh)
    return out
```
Random smooth:
```
def _random_smooth_at(n,rng,alpha):
    h=rng.standard_normal(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(h)
    cut=max(1,int(n*0.1))
    A[cut:] *= np.exp(-np.arange(len(A)-cut)*(0.5/cut)) # smooth
    low=np.fft.irfft(A,n)
    h=1.0 + alpha*np.tanh(low)
    h=np.maximum(h,0.01)
    if rng.random()<0.5:
       f=int(rng.integers(2,10))
       h = h * (0.2 + 0.8*np.abs(np.sin(np.pi*(np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)) + 0.1*np.random? no)) )
    return _normalize(h)
```

In `_normalize`, if arr has inf, filter? We'll clip. Implementation:
```
def _normalize(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    orig_n=arr.size
    arr=arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    if arr.size==0: return np.ones(orig_n)/orig_n
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,None)
    s=arr.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(arr.size)/arr.size
    return arr/s
```
If filtering changes length, subsequent n changes. For parent, okay. For arithmetic candidate, should not have nonfinite; if it does, resetting length maybe bad. But better than fail.

Now, one potential problem: `_candidate_key` prioritizes actual. If actual evaluation has random noise? no. If actual not available (inf), internal. Good.

Now, final fallback: If key best is parent and best_seq is original, normalize final. If best actual finite but internal inf? no.

Let's write. Use `np.inf` finite check.

One more improvement: Use actual evaluate_sequence to choose beta? no.

Let's final.
</think>

```python
"""
Full-convolution peak minimizer.

Minimizes score(a) = 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2 by optimizing on the
simplex sum(a)=1.  The main ideas are:
- smoothing (log-sum-exp) of the maximum convolution coefficient,
- matrix-method / linearized cross-correlation mixing:
    given p, minimize max conv(p,q) over q on the simplex, then mix p <- (1-t)p+tq,
- nesterov-style projected subgradient for the self objective,
- smoothed L-BFGS in log/softmax coordinates for final polishing,
- diverse starts and multi-length polish to escape local minima.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _next_pow2(n):
    k = 1
    while k < n:
        k <<= 1
    return max(2, k)


def _fft_convolve(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = b.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = n + m - 1
    if out_len <= 2048:
        return np.convolve(a, b)
    N = _next_pow2(out_len)
    try:
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
        return np.fft.irfft(A * B, N)[:out_len]
    except Exception:
        return np.convolve(a, b)


def _normalize(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    orig_n = arr.size
    arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(orig_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(orig_n)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def _project_simplex(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * idx > (cssv - 1.0)
    rho = np.where(cond)[0]
    if rho.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    r = int(rho[-1])
    theta = (cssv[r] - 1.0) / (r + 1.0)
    return np.maximum(x - theta, 0.0)


def _score_full(p):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = _fft_convolve(p, p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def _actual_score(seq):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
        if not lst:
            return np.inf
        val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        val = float(val)
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            return np.inf
        return val
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def _candidate_key(actual, internal):
    if np.isfinite(actual):
        return (0, float(actual), float(internal))
    return (1, float(internal), 0.0)


def _resample(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == n:
        return _normalize(a)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return _normalize(out)


def _load_parent():
    try:
        obj = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0:
            return None
        arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
        if arr.size == 0:
            return None
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            return None
        return arr / s
    except Exception:
        return None


def _random_smooth_at(n, rng, alpha):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(noise)
    freq = np.arange(A.size, dtype=np.float64)
    decay = np.exp(-freq * (0.7 / max(1.0, 0.08 * n)))
    A = A * decay
    low = np.fft.irfft(A, n)
    sd = float(np.std(low))
    if sd < 1e-12:
        sd = 1.0
    h = 1.0 + alpha * np.tanh(low / sd)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.02)
    return _normalize(h)


def _generate_starts(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    starts = []

    starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    starts.append(x + 0.01)
    starts.append((1.0 - x) + 0.01)
    starts.append(x * x + 0.01)
    starts.append((1.0 - x) * (1.0 - x) + 0.01)
    starts.append((x - 0.5) * (x - 0.5) + 0.01)

    for width in (0.08, 0.15, 0.25):
        h = (
            0.05
            + np.exp(-((x / width) ** 2))
            + np.exp(-(((1.0 - x) / width) ** 2))
        )
        starts.append(h)

    for f in (3, 5, 7, 11, 17, 29):
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        h = 1.0 + 0.85 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * x + ph)
        starts.append(np.maximum(h, 0.02))

    for k in range(5):
        h = _random_smooth_at(n, rng, 0.55 + 0.18 * k)
        starts.append(h)

    out = []
    for h in starts:
        hh = _normalize(h)
        if hh.size == n:
            out.append(hh)
    return out


def _logsp_weights(u, beta):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    if u.size == 0:
        return 0.0, 1.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    maxu = float(np.max(u))
    shifted = (u - maxu) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(u)
        w[int(np.argmax(u))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / s
    return maxu, s, w


def _corr_weights(w, p):
    """
    h[j] = sum_k w[k] * p[k-j], valid indices, for p length n.
    Computed as conv(w, reversed(p))[n-1:2n-1].
    """
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = _fft_convolve(w, p[::-1])
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    start = n - 1
    stop = start + n
    if out.size >= n:
        lo = max(0, start)
        hi = min(out.size, stop)
        if hi > lo:
            h[: hi - lo] = out[lo:hi]
    return h


def _self_smooth_z_value(z, beta):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return 1e100
    a = _softmax(z)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100
    u = n * _fft_convolve(a, a)
    if u.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    maxu, s, _ = _logsp_weights(u, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return float(beta * maxu)
    return float(beta * (maxu + np.log(s)))


def _self_smooth_z_grad(z, beta):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.zeros_like(z)
    a = _softmax(z)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros_like(z)
    u = n * _fft_convolve(a, a)
    if u.size == 0:
        return np.zeros_like(z)
    _, _, w = _logsp_weights(u, beta)
    h = _corr_weights(w, a)
    g = 2.0 * float(n) * h
    ag = float(np.dot(g, a))
    gz = a * (g - ag)
    gz = gz - np.mean(gz)
    return gz


def _lbfgs_self(p_init, deadline, min_rem):
    p = _normalize(p_init)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return p, _score_full(p_init)
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_full(p)
    betas = (0.10, 0.05, 0.025, 0.0125, 0.00625, 0.003125)
    for i, beta in enumerate(betas):
        rem = deadline - min_rem - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 30.0:
            break
        z = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
        z -= float(np.mean(z))
        # Conservative iteration count: each L-BFGS step is expensive for n ~ 16k.
        max_iter = int(max(30, min(300, rem / 1.0)))
        max_iter = int(min(max_iter, 320 - 30 * i))
        if max_iter < 20:
            max_iter = 20
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _self_smooth_z_value,
                z,
                jac=_self_smooth_z_grad,
                args=(beta,),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": max_iter,
                    "ftol": 1e-14,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "maxcor": 20,
                    "maxls": 20,
                },
            )
            a_new = _softmax(res.x)
            sc_new = _score_full(a_new)
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best = a_new.copy()
                best_sc = sc_new
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best, best_sc


def _self_subgrad(p_init, deadline, min_rem, max_iter=4000, beta=0.05, seed=0):
    p = _normalize(p_init).copy()
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return p, np.inf
    best = p.copy()
    u = n * _fft_convolve(p, p)
    best_m = float(np.max(u)) if u.size else 0.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    lr = 0.025
    momentum = 0.82
    beta_cur = beta
    no_improve = 0
    stalls = 0

    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.monotonic() + min_rem > deadline:
            break
        u = n * _fft_convolve(p, p)
        if u.size == 0:
            break
        maxu, s, w = _logsp_weights(u, beta_cur)
        h = _corr_weights(w, p)
        g = 2.0 * float(n) * h
        tang = g - float(np.mean(g))
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(tang))
        if gn < 1e-15:
            break
        step = lr / max(gn, 1e-15)
        if step > 0.04:
            step = 0.04
        v = momentum * v - step * tang
        vn = float(np.linalg.norm(v))
        if vn > 0.18:
            v *= 0.18 / vn
        cand = p + v
        cand = _project_simplex(cand)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
            cand = _normalize(p + 0.001 * rng.standard_normal(n))
        p = cand

        beta_cur = max(0.004, beta_cur * 0.9965)
        if (it + 1) % 5 == 0:
            u2 = n * _fft_convolve(p, p)
            m2 = float(np.max(u2)) if u2.size else 0.0
            if m2 < best_m - 1e-14:
                best_m = m2
                best = p.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 160:
                stalls += 1
                if stalls > 10:
                    break
                pert = 0.0015 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                cand2 = _project_simplex(p + pert)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(cand2)):
                    cand2 = _normalize(p)
                u3 = n * _fft_convolve(cand2, cand2)
                m3 = float(np.max(u3)) if u3.size else 0.0
                if np.isfinite(m3) and m3 < best_m:
                    best_m = m3
                    best = cand2.copy()
                p = cand2.copy()
                v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                lr = 0.025
                beta_cur = max(0.03, beta_cur)
                no_improve = 0
    return best, 2.0 * best_m


def _cross_min(p_fixed, max_iter, beta, deadline, min_rem, seed=0):
    p = _normalize(p_fixed)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return p, 0.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    starts = []
    starts.append(p.copy())
    starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    starts.append(np.maximum(x + 0.02, 0.02))
    starts.append(np.maximum((1.0 - x) + 0.02, 0.02))
    starts.append(_random_smooth_at(n, rng, 0.75))
    starts.append(_random_smooth_at(n, rng, 1.05))

    it_per = max(25, max_iter // max(1, len(starts)))
    best_q = p.copy()
    best_x = np.inf

    for si, q0 in enumerate(starts):
        q = _normalize(q0).copy()
        lr = 0.022
        beta_cur = beta
        cur_max = np.inf
        no_improve = 0
        for it in range(it_per):
            if time.monotonic() + min_rem > deadline:
                break
            u = n * _fft_convolve(p, q)
            if u.size == 0:
                break
            maxu, s, w = _logsp_weights(u, beta_cur)
            if maxu < cur_max - 1e-14:
                cur_max = maxu
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve > 35:
                    lr *= 0.7
                    no_improve = 0
                    if lr < 5e-4:
                        break
            h = _corr_weights(w, p)
            g = float(n) * h
            tang = g - float(np.mean(g))
            gn = float(np.linalg.norm(tang))
            if gn < 1e-15:
                break
            step = lr / max(gn, 1e-15)
            if step > 0.045:
                step = 0.045
            q = _project_simplex(q - step * tang)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
                q = _normalize(p)
            beta_cur = max(0.008, beta_cur * 0.975)

        u = n * _fft_convolve(p, q)
        mx = float(np.max(u)) if u.size else 0.0
        if np.isfinite(mx) and mx < best_x:
            best_x = mx
            best_q = q.copy()
        if time.monotonic() + min_rem > deadline:
            break

    if not np.isfinite(best_x):
        u = n * _fft_convolve(p, p)
        best_x = float(np.max(u)) if u.size else 0.0
        best_q = p.copy()
    return best_q, float(best_x)


def _mix_best_full(p, q, current_m):
    p = _normalize(p)
    q = _normalize(q)
    n = p.size
    best = p.copy()
    best_m = current_m
    for t in (0.05, 0.10, 0.18, 0.30, 0.50, 0.80, 1.00):
        b = (1.0 - t) * p + t * q
        b = _project_simplex(b)
        u = n * _fft_convolve(b, b)
        m = float(np.max(u)) if u.size else 0.0
        if np.isfinite(m) and m < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m = m
            best = b.copy()
    return best, best_m


def _cross_mm_loop(p_init, deadline, min_rem, seed=0, max_outer=24, inner_it=70, beta=0.085):
    p = _normalize(p_init).copy()
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return p, np.inf
    u = n * _fft_convolve(p, p)
    current_m = float(np.max(u)) if u.size else 0.0
    best = p.copy()
    best_m = current_m
    no_improve = 0

    for outer in range(max_outer):
        if time.monotonic() + min_rem > deadline:
            break
        q, xmin = _cross_min(p, inner_it, beta, deadline, min_rem, seed + outer)
        b, bm = _mix_best_full(p, q, current_m)
        if bm < best_m - 1e-14:
            best_m = bm
            best = b.copy()
            p = b.copy()
            current_m = bm
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 4:
                break
            # small perturb to give another cross direction a chance
            rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 1000 + outer)
            cand = _project_simplex(p + 0.001 * rng.standard_normal(n))
            u2 = n * _fft_convolve(cand, cand)
            m2 = float(np.max(u2)) if u2.size else np.inf
            if np.isfinite(m2) and m2 < best_m:
                best_m = m2
                best = cand.copy()
                p = cand.copy()
                current_m = m2
    return best, 2.0 * best_m


def _cross_smooth_z_value(z, beta, p_fixed):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return 1e100
    q = _softmax(z)
    n = p_fixed.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100
    u = n * _fft_convolve(p_fixed, q)
    if u.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    maxu, s, _ = _logsp_weights(u, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return float(beta * maxu)
    return float(beta * (maxu + np.log(s)))


def _cross_smooth_z_grad(z, beta, p_fixed):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.zeros_like(z)
    q = _softmax(z)
    n = p_fixed.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros_like(z)
    u = n * _fft_convolve(p_fixed, q)
    if u.size == 0:
        return np.zeros_like(z)
    _, _, w = _logsp_weights(u, beta)
    h = _corr_weights(w, p_fixed)
    g = float(n) * h
    ag = float(np.dot(g, q))
    gz = q * (g - ag)
    gz = gz - np.mean(gz)
    return gz


def _lbfgs_cross(p_fixed, q_init, deadline, min_rem, beta=0.05, max_iter=120):
    p_fixed = _normalize(p_fixed)
    q = _normalize(q_init)
    n = p_fixed.size
    if n <= 0:
        return q, np.inf
    rem = deadline - min_rem - time.monotonic()
    if rem <= 10.0:
        return q, np.inf
    z = np.log(np.maximum(q, 1e-300))
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _cross_smooth_z_value,
            z,
            jac=_cross_smooth_z_grad,
            args=(beta, p_fixed),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": max_iter,
                "ftol": 1e-14,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "maxcor": 16,
                "maxls": 16,
            },
        )
        q2 = _softmax(res.x)
        u = n * _fft_convolve(p_fixed, q2)
        mx = float(np.max(u)) if u.size else np.inf
        u0 = n * _fft_convolve(p_fixed, q)
        mx0 = float(np.max(u0)) if u0.size else np.inf
        if np.isfinite(mx) and mx < mx0 - 1e-14:
            return q2, mx
    except Exception:
        pass
    u0 = n * _fft_convolve(p_fixed, q)
    return q, float(np.max(u0)) if u0.size else np.inf


def _cross_mm_loop_refined(p_init, deadline, min_rem, seed=0, max_outer=24, inner_it=70, beta=0.085):
    p = _normalize(p_init).copy()
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return p, np.inf
    u = n * _fft_convolve(p, p)
    current_m = float(np.max(u)) if u.size else 0.0
    best = p.copy()
    best_m = current_m
    no_improve = 0

    for outer in range(max_outer):
        if time.monotonic() + min_rem > deadline:
            break

        q, xmin = _cross_min(p, inner_it, beta, deadline, min_rem, seed + outer)

        # For the first few outer moves, spend a short L-BFGS budget on the
        # convex cross subproblem.  This often gives a much better q direction.
        if outer < 4 and time.monotonic() + 90.0 < deadline:
            q2, mx2 = _lbfgs_cross(
                p,
                q,
                deadline - 60.0,
                30.0,
                beta=0.05,
                max_iter=90,
            )
            if np.isfinite(mx2) and mx2 < xmin - 1e-14:
                q = q2
                xmin = mx2

        b, bm = _mix_best_full(p, q, current_m)
        if bm < best_m - 1e-14:
            best_m = bm
            best = b.copy()
            p = b.copy()
            current_m = bm
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 5:
                break
            rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 5555 + outer)
            cand = _project_simplex(p + 0.0012 * rng.standard_normal(n))
            u2 = n * _fft_convolve(cand, cand)
            m2 = float(np.max(u2)) if u2.size else np.inf
            if np.isfinite(m2) and m2 < best_m:
                best_m = m2
                best = cand.copy()
                p = cand.copy()
                current_m = m2
    return best, 2.0 * best_m


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=1800):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        B = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        B = 1800.0
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, B - 25.0)
    seed = int(seed) % (2**63 - 1)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = _load_parent()

    n_base = 16384
    if parent is not None:
        lp = parent.size
        if 4096 <= lp <= 32768:
            n_base = lp
        elif lp < 4096:
            n_base = 4096
        else:
            n_base = 16384

    starts = []
    if parent is not None:
        starts.append(_resample(parent, n_base))
    starts.extend(_generate_starts(n_base, rng))

    scored = []
    for idx, a in enumerate(starts):
        sc = _score_full(a)
        scored.append((sc, idx, a))
    scored.sort()

    if scored:
        best_seq = scored[0][2].copy()
        best_int = scored[0][0]
    else:
        best_seq = np.ones(n_base, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_base)
        best_int = _score_full(best_seq)

    best_act = _actual_score(best_seq)
    best_key = _candidate_key(best_act, best_int)

    # Preserve the original parent as a candidate, in case resampling lost detail.
    if parent is not None:
        p_orig = _normalize(parent)
        sc_orig = _score_full(p_orig)
        act_orig = _actual_score(p_orig)
        key_orig = _candidate_key(act_orig, sc_orig)
        if key_orig < best_key:
            best_seq = p_orig.copy()
            best_int = sc_orig
            best_act = act_orig
            best_key = key_orig

    # Main mixed search cycle.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 600.0:
        phase_deadline = min(deadline - 600.0, time.monotonic() + 620.0)
        a_new, sc_new = _cross_mm_loop_refined(
            best_seq, phase_deadline, 60.0, seed=seed,
            max_outer=14, inner_it=60, beta=0.085
        )
        act_new = _actual_score(a_new) if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0 else np.inf
        key_new = _candidate_key(act_new, sc_new)
        if key_new < best_key:
            best_seq, best_int, best_act, best_key = a_new.copy(), sc_new, act_new, key_new

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 420.0:
        phase_deadline = min(deadline - 420.0, time.monotonic() + 300.0)
        a_new, sc_new = _self_subgrad(
            best_seq, phase_deadline, 60.0,
            max_iter=2500, beta=0.05, seed=seed + 11
        )
        act_new = _actual_score(a_new) if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0 else np.inf
        key_new = _candidate_key(act_new, sc_new)
        if key_new < best_key:
            best_seq, best_int, best_act, best_key = a_new.copy(), sc_new, act_new, key_new

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 320.0:
        phase_deadline = min(deadline - 320.0, time.monotonic() + 320.0)
        a_new, sc_new = _lbfgs_self(best_seq, phase_deadline, 60.0)
        act_new = _actual_score(a_new) if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0 else np.inf
        key_new = _candidate_key(act_new, sc_new)
        if key_new < best_key:
            best_seq, best_int, best_act, best_key = a_new.copy(), sc_new, act_new, key_new

    # Second round: cross-then-self polishing.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 300.0:
        phase_deadline = min(deadline - 300.0, time.monotonic() + 220.0)
        a_new, sc_new = _cross_mm_loop_refined(
            best_seq, phase_deadline, 60.0, seed=seed + 100,
            max_outer=12, inner_it=55, beta=0.075
        )
        act_new = _actual_score(a_new) if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0 else np.inf
        key_new = _candidate_key(act_new, sc_new)
        if key_new < best_key:
            best_seq, best_int, best_act, best_key = a_new.copy(), sc_new, act_new, key_new

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 200.0:
        phase_deadline = min(deadline - 200.0, time.monotonic() + 140.0)
        a_new, sc_new = _self_subgrad(
            best_seq, phase_deadline, 60.0,
            max_iter=2200, beta=0.04, seed=seed + 211
        )
        act_new = _actual_score(a_new) if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0 else np.inf
        key_new = _candidate_key(act_new, sc_new)
        if key_new < best_key:
            best_seq, best_int, best_act, best_key = a_new.copy(), sc_new, act_new, key_new

    # Try a few alternate global starts, briefly.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 260.0 and len(scored) > 1:
        for rank in range(1, min(4, len(scored))):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 160.0:
                break
            a_try = scored[rank][2].copy()
            if a_try.size == 0:
                continue
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 160.0:
                a_try, sc_try = _cross_mm_loop_refined(
                    a_try, min(deadline - 160.0, time.monotonic() + 80.0), 60.0,
                    seed=seed + 500 + rank, max_outer=6, inner_it=35, beta=0.08
                )
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
                a_try, sc_try = _lbfgs_self(a_try, min(deadline - 120.0, time.monotonic() + 70.0), 50.0)
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 100.0:
                a_try, sc_try = _self_subgrad(
                    a_try, min(deadline - 100.0, time.monotonic() + 50.0), 50.0,
                    max_iter=900, beta=0.045, seed=seed + 700 + rank
                )
            act_try = _actual_score(a_try) if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0 else np.inf
            key_try = _candidate_key(act_try, sc_try)
            if key_try < best_key:
                best_seq, best_int, best_act, best_key = a_try, sc_try, act_try, key_try

    # Try nearby lengths.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0:
        cur_n = best_seq.size
        for n2 in (8192, 12288, 24576, 4096):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
                break
            if n2 == cur_n:
                continue
            a2 = _resample(best_seq, n2)
            if a2.size == 0:
                continue
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 160.0:
                a2, sc2 = _self_subgrad(
                    a2, min(deadline - 160.0, time.monotonic() + 90.0), 50.0,
                    max_iter=1500, beta=0.05, seed=seed + n2
                )
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
                a2, sc2 = _lbfgs_self(a2, min(deadline - 120.0, time.monotonic() + 80.0), 45.0)
            act2 = _actual_score(a2) if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0 else np.inf
            key2 = _candidate_key(act2, sc2)
            if key2 < best_key:
                best_seq, best_int, best_act, best_key = a2, sc2, act2, key2

    # Final very short polish on the selected best.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        a_fin, sc_fin = _self_subgrad(
            best_seq,
            min(deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic() + 20.0),
            5.0,
            max_iter=800,
            beta=0.035,
            seed=seed + 9999,
        )
        act_fin = _actual_score(a_fin) if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0 else np.inf
        key_fin = _candidate_key(act_fin, sc_fin)
        if key_fin < best_key:
            best_seq, best_int, best_act, best_key = a_fin, sc_fin, act_fin, key_fin

    final = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    final = final[np.isfinite(final)]
    if final.size == 0:
        final = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(final.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.01:
        final = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        final = final / s

    out = [float(x) for x in final]
    act_out = _actual_score(out)
    if not np.isfinite(act_out):
        fallback = [1.0] * 64
        out = fallback
    return out
```
